Public Information Statement
National Weather Service San Francisco Bay Area
611 PM PDT Sat Mar 28 2026

...San Francisco Bay Area NOAA Weather Radio All Hazards Station
Will Be Off The Air Until Further Notice...

NOAA Weather Radio All Hazards transmitter KHB-49 operating on a
frequency of 162.400 MHz will be off the air until further
notice due to technical difficulties.

During the outage, listeners may be able to receive NOAA Weather 
Radio broadcasts on frequencies 162.550 MHz from Mount Umunhum,
162.475 MHz from Sonoma County, or 162.425 MHz from Mount Diablo.
